# TODO.md — live phase tracker

Last updated: 2026-09-27. **Work top to bottom, one unchecked item at a time.** Don't skip ahead — see `CLAUDE.md`'s "one rule that prevents scatter". Full detail for every item lives in `QURAN_COMPANION_ROADMAP.md`; this file only tracks status.

## 🧭 الخطة الرئيسية حتى نهاية المشروع (2026-09-27) — المرجع الوحيد لـ«ما التالي؟»

**القاعدة (إسماعيل 2026-09-27):** «نعمل بشكل متسلسل ومنتظم ودون تشتت، لا نهتم بجهة وندع جهة». مرحلة واحدة فقط في كل وقت، من الأعلى للأسفل. لا تبدأ مرحلة قبل أن يتحقق **شرط انتهاء** السابقة (بناء + `flutter analyze` نظيف + اختبارات المرحلة + تحقق على هاتف إسماعيل حين يمسّ الواجهة). الطلب الجديد يُضاف إلى مكانه في هذه القائمة، ولا يقطع المرحلة الجارية إلا إن كان عطلًا يمنع الاستخدام.

كل ما تحت هذا القسم في الملف **أرشيف** (تاريخ المراحل السابقة وتفاصيلها)، ويُقرأ للرجوع فقط. تفاصيل كل مسار في وثيقته المذكورة.

- إقلاع بلا انتظار + شاشة بداية فاخرة (`docs/architecture/ZERO_WAIT_PROGRESSIVE_ARCHITECTURE.md`) — Home من 31 ث إلى ~1 ث.
- نظام الاستجابة للضغط IF-1..IF-5 (`docs/architecture/INTERACTION_FEEDBACK_ARCHITECTURE.md`) + حديث/فائدة متجددة في كل دخول.
- الحجم S1–S2: lite arm64 من 459 إلى 307.8 MB. مهارة `lightweight-feature-rules` قاعدة إلزامية.

# 📚 الأرشيف (للرجوع فقط — لا يحدد الأولوية)

## قضايا معروفة (منفصلة عن أي مرحلة أعلاه)

- **2026-09-16 — `flutter test` الكامل يتعلّق (hang حقيقي، لا بطء)**: عند تشغيل المجموعة **كاملة** فقط، `test/akhlaq_screens_test.dart`'s `tearDownAll` يتجمّد (CPU يتوقف فعليًا عن التقدّم) — على الأرجح تسرّب مورد (Timer/isolate/اشتراك) من ملف سابق في الترتيب (لوحظ يحدث مباشرة بعد `test/ayah_notebook_screen_test.dart`)، لا خلل في `akhlaq_screens_test.dart` نفسه (يمرّ بنجاح في ~6 ثوانٍ عند تشغيله منفردًا: `flutter test test/akhlaq_screens_test.dart`). تحقّقتُ بدقة (`git stash` + إعادة التشغيل): نفس التعليق بالضبط بوجود أي تعديل حديث وبدونه — عطل بنية اختبارات موجود مسبقًا، غير مرتبط بأي تغيير معيّن. لم يُصلَح بعد — مهمة منفصلة تحتاج عزل الملف/الملفات المسبِّبة للتسرّب. إلى حين إصلاحه: استخدم `flutter test <ملف محدد>` بدل التشغيل الكامل، أو تحقّق عبر `flutter analyze` + تشغيل معزول للملفات ذات الصلة بالتغيير.

- [ ] **SIZE-7 — native/dependency audit:** تقليل الاعتماديات وتفعيل shrinking فقط بعد قياس واختبار plugins.

## جدول العمل — الأهم فالأهم (2026-08-26)

**حالة Phase 71.7 الآن**: إسماعيل يجمع صفحات المصحف بشكل موازٍ (226 من 604 حتى الآن) ولا ينتظر — طلب صراحة أن يستمر العمل على أشياء أخرى حتى يُكمل. **لا شيء أدناه ينتظر اكتمال الـ604 صفحة.** إن أراد تسليم الصفحات على دفعات بدل انتظار الاكتمال، نمط 71.7 (نسخ SVG + JSON إلى `assets/quran/mushaf_borders/`، تحديث `_mushafBorderPages`) مُثبَت ومُكرَّر فعليًا — قرار التوقيت له هو، لا افتراض هنا.

**المستوى 1 — جاهز الآن فعليًا، بلا أي قرار معلَّق، ابدأ بهذا الترتيب**:
1. **72.5** — بحث موحّد (قرآن+تفاسير) — صغيرة، محلية بالكامل، تُنهي المرحلة 72 المتبقية.
2. **77.2 → 77.3** — نموذج البيانات ثم شاشة اليوم — مُفكَّكة لآخر خطوة فعلاً (migration v45 محدَّد بالاسم والسطر)، هذا التزام "غدًا نبدأ".
3. **76.1** — تلوين التجويد — بيانات مفتوحة حقيقية جاهزة، قيمة عالية، لا سيرفر.
4. **73.1a-e** — بحث محتوى مدرّب المناظرة فقط (لا كود، لا سيرفر) — يمكن إنجازه بالتوازي مع أي مما سبق، لا يتعارض مع تسلسل "القرآن أولاً" لأن 71/72 لا تحتاج توقّفًا كاملاً لبدء بحث محتوى منفصل.

**المستوى 2 — جاهز لكن يحتاج قرارًا صغيرًا من إسماعيل أولاً (اسأل عند الوصول، لا تخمّن)**:
5. **77.5 (جزء الكتب)** — يحتاج بحثًا حقيقيًا لكتاب "البداية والنهاية" فقط (البقية موثَّقة أصلاً في الرودماب).
6. **77.11b/c** — ربط التطبيق بالسكربت — جاهز تقنيًا، ينتظر فقط أن تُبنى 77.2/77.3 أولاً (تبعية طبيعية، لا قرار خارجي).
7. **76.3a** — فحص التلاوة — ينتظر تأكيد إسماعيل الصريح أن نموذج تعرّف كلام محلي مختلف عن قاعدة "لا AI حي".

**المستوى 3 — أكبر حجمًا، يبدأ بعد المستوى 1+2، بترتيبه الخاص**:
8. **76.2** — كلمة بكلمة (يحتاج مصدر بيانات مرخَّص، بحث مستقل).
9. **77.12 + 74.0** — بوت تليجرام/n8n — يحتاج قرار بنية تحتية واحد يخدم الاثنين معًا.
10. **74 كاملة** — منصة المساجد — بعد 73، وبعد تأكيد 74.0 صراحة.

**المستوى 4 — مستمر/لاحق، لا يُجدوَل كخطوة واحدة**:
- **75** — بحث جودة دوري، يُراجَع كلما وُجد MVP حقيقي جديد.
- **78** — مشروع المحتوى — منفصل عن هذا المستودع، ينتظر قرار إسماعيل الصريح بالبدء فيه تحديدًا.

**77.6 (عملاء ERP) مُهمَّش صراحة بقرار إسماعيل — آخر ما يُبنى، أبسط نسخة فقط، لا يُعطَّل عليه أي شيء آخر مهما كان الترتيب أعلاه.**

**ملف السكربت جاهز الآن، لا حاجة لإعادة إعطائه**: `google_apps_script/life_plan_sync.gs` — الصقه في شيتك (Extensions → Apps Script)، عدّل `CONFIG.START_DATE`، شغّل `setupRealCheckboxes` مرة واحدة، ثم Deploy وفعّل الـ Trigger (التعليمات كاملة أعلى الملف نفسه).

## معمارية n8n — الصورة الأكبر (2026-08-26، توضيح مهم من إسماعيل)

**n8n هو "الأم"، وطالب العلم فرع فيه** — ليس العكس. المشروع الأكبر عند إسماعيل هو منصة أتمتة شخصية موحّدة (n8n كمركز)، تتفرّع منها عدة أنظمة: تطبيق طالب العلم (القرآن والحياة اليومية)، خطة الحياة الشاملة (شيت جوجل + بوت تليجرام، جاهزة اليوم)، ومستقبلًا نظام إنتاج المحتوى/السوشل ميديا (Phase 78 أسفل)، وربما ERP والبحث عن عمل لاحقًا. هذا المستودع (`TalibAlIlmApp`) يبني **فرعًا واحدًا فقط** من هذه المنصة — التخطيط هنا يخص الفرع، لا المنصة كلها، حتى لا يختلط نطاق العمل.

**الاتصال الرباعي الحي** (تطوير على "الاتصال الثلاثي" السابق في 77.12): التطبيق + الشيت + بوت تليجرام + **n8n** — كل الأربعة متصلة، وn8n هو من يشغّل/يراقب/يطوّر الاتصال بينها كلما كان يعمل (لا اتصال دائم مطلوب من التطبيق نفسه — n8n هو الطبقة التي تبقى نشطة).

**دور Claude**: إسماعيل يريد Claude "مديرًا" على n8n — أي أن القرارات والتوجيه لمسارات n8n (أي workflow يُبنى، بأي ترتيب، وبأي معايير أمان/جودة) تأتي من Claude، وn8n ينفّذ. هذا يعني عمليًا: عند العمل الفعلي على n8n (خارج نطاق هذا المستودع تحديدًا)، تُطبَّق نفس انضباط "خطّط أولًا، تحقق من الحقائق، لا تخمّن" المُتَّبع طوال هذه الجلسة، وليس تفويضًا مفتوحًا لأتمتة أي شيء دون مراجعة.

## Phase 77 — خلاصة سريعة للتنفيذ (اقرأ هذا أولاً، التفاصيل الكاملة أسفل)

**الهدف بجملة واحدة**: التطبيق يتابعني يوميًا (جدول 23 خانة زمنية) بدل الإكسل، يتزامن مع شيتي الحقيقي (سكربت جاهز وشغّال فعليًا اليوم)، ويرسل تنبيهات/تقارير عبر بوت تليجرام (لاحقًا عبر n8n)، ليساعدني أنجز خطتي الحقيقية: القرآن، الكتب الخمسة، والدخل (هدف 1500$/شهر).

**الجاهز فعلاً، لا تُعِد بناءه**: `google_apps_script/life_plan_sync.gs` — يعمل حي، مُختبر على الرابط الحقيقي، يحدّث "تتبع الإنجاز" تلقائيًا من "الجدول اليومي".

**ترتيب البناء الفعلي** (واحدة تلو الأخرى، لا تتخطَّ):
1. **77.2** — نموذج بيانات Flutter/sqflite (migration v45) — انظر الخطوات الذرية الكاملة أسفل، لا حاجة للتفكير فيها مرة أخرى.
2. **77.3** — شاشة اليوم في التطبيق (23 خانة، تُنجز فيها فعليًا).
3. **77.5** — القرآن+الكتب: وصل بمحرك الحفظ الموجود + مكتبة `QURAN_COMPANION_ROADMAP.md` §4.9 (4 من 5 كتب مخطَّط لها هناك أصلاً).
4. **77.11b/c** — ربط التطبيق بالسكربت (قراءة ثم كتابة).
5. **77.12** — بوت تليجرام + n8n (الاتصال الرباعي).
6. باقي المحاور (77.6-77.10) عند الحاجة الفعلية فقط؛ ERP (77.6) آخر شيء، أبسط نسخة، لا يُعطَّل عليه شيء.

**قيود ثابتة لا تتغيّر مهما طال العمل**: لا AI حي يواجه المستخدم؛ بلا سيرفر تقليدي مُستأجر (n8n/Apps Script/Firebase فقط)؛ "خاص أولًا وعام حوله" — لا تقيّد العمق من أجل عمومية مستقبلية افتراضية؛ لا أرقام مالية مُختلَقة — الهدف 1500$/شهر ينتظر أرقام إسماعيل الحقيقية.

**قرارات معلَّقة تحتاج إسماعيل قبل نقاط محدَّدة** (لا تخمّن، لا تتوقف عن العمل، تخطَّ فقط ما يعتمد عليها): تاريخ START_DATE الحقيقي (77.1d)، تأكيد الصف 18 في PILLAR_ROW_MAP (77.1b)، الأرقام المالية الحقيقية لعملاء ERP (قبل أي مسار نحو 1500$).

## Phase 77 — "رفيق الدرب" personal life companion (pilot: Ismail's own 90-day plan) — 📋 PLANNED, not started

Ismail's request 2026-08-25: he shared his own real, existing life-management spreadsheet (`خطة_الحياة_الشاملة`, Google Sheets — 10 tabs, fetched and confirmed: 🏠 Dashboard, 📅 الجدول اليومي, 📖 القرآن والكتب, 🎬 السوشل ميديا, 🏪 ERP والعملاء, 💼 Microworkers والتوظيف, 💻 Python والبرمجة, 📊 تتبع الإنجاز, 🌟 صدقة جارية, ⚡ قواعد النجاح) and said plainly he built it but never followed through on it ("هذا ما كنت أريد أن أنجزه لكن تكاسلت"). He wants the app to become the real accountability companion that gets him through it — daily schedule tracking, a Telegram bot that messages him when he completes tasks and lets him act via buttons, completions saved back to his real Google Sheet, reminders. Explicit design instruction: build it as a **general app feature any user could set up**, not something hardcoded to Ismail — but he is the real pilot user, testing it on himself for 90 real days before it's considered for anyone else ("لنجربه بي أولًا").

**Design principle — revised 2026-08-26 per Ismail's direct correction, this supersedes the original wording below**: "خاص أولًا، وعام من حوله" — build deep and fully unconstrained for Ismail's *actual* 90-day plan first; never hold back a real feature or a piece of depth just because a hypothetical future user "might not need it." Genericness is a wrapper applied *around* something real and proven, not a ceiling imposed on it from day one. Concretely: the `life_pillars`/`life_tasks`/etc. tables (77.2) stay reusable *shapes* — that part doesn't change, it's just good schema design — but every screen, automation, and integration in 77.3-77.14 gets built to make Ismail's real plan succeed at full depth, not trimmed down to a lowest-common-denominator "any user" version. Whether/how this becomes a general onboarding flow for other users is a decision for **after** 77.13's 90-day pilot proves it actually works on one real person (already the plan) — it is explicitly *not* a constraint to design around before that point.

*(Original 2026-08-25 wording, kept for context, now qualified by the above)*: this is one configurable system, not seven hardcoded ones. A "pillar" (محور), a "task" (مهمة), a time-slot schedule, and a completion log are generic concepts — Ismail's specific 7 pillars (Quran+books, social media, ERP clients, Python, Microworkers/job search, sport/food/other, sleep) are *his* seed data, not the schema. Two of his pillars (Quran+books memorization, achievement/certificates) should plug into features **already built** in this app (the memorization/review engine, `milestone_repository.dart`, `certificates_screen.dart`) rather than being reimplemented — reuse before rebuild, same discipline as every other phase.

**الهدف المالي الصريح (2026-08-26، إسماعيل)**: دخل شهري لا يقل عن 1500$ أونلاين — ليس المطلوب تحقيقه الشهر القادم، بل تقدّم حقيقي وواضح نحوه، خطوة خطوة، لا صبر بلا فائدة. **بصراحة تامة**: لا يمكن بناء خارطة طريق رقمية حقيقية لهذا الهدف الآن — شيت إسماعيل نفسه لا يزال يضع "$X/شهر" كقيمة مؤقتة لكل عميل ERP (لم تُستبدل بأرقام حقيقية بعد). الأرقام الوهمية أسوأ من لا أرقام. **ما هو حقيقي ومعروف الآن**: مصادر الدخل الثلاثة في خطته (عملاء ERP — نمو 7→25 خلال 6 أشهر مخطَّط له أصلاً في شيته؛ Microworkers — تقديريًا $10-50 لكل مهمة، 5 مهام/أسبوع؛ التوظيف/الاستشارات) هي الأساس الواقعي القريب المدى؛ محتوى Phase 78 مساهم بعيد المدى فقط، ليس مصدر دخل قريب. **الخطوة التالية الحقيقية لهذا الهدف تحديدًا**: عندما تصل الخطة إلى 77.6 (ولو بأبسط نسخة، رغم تأجيله)، يحتاج إسماعيل استبدال كل "$X/شهر" في شيته بأرقامه الحقيقية — عندها فقط يصبح ممكنًا حساب مسار رقمي صادق نحو 1500$، لا قبل ذلك.

## Known issues — not yet fixed (check here before assuming something new broke)

- [ ] **Quran search returns no results for real words** — Ismail reported searching "الظالمون" in "البحث في القرآن" gave "لم يعثر على نتائج", though that word is genuinely common in the Quran. Not yet root-caused — suspect is `normalizeArabicForSearch`/the `text_normalized` index (`lib/utils/arabic_normalize.dart`, `lib/repositories/quran_search_repository.dart`) possibly not matching the AmiriQuran-rendered text's exact Unicode form, but this needs real investigation, not a guess-fix.
- [ ] **`svg2` pages 121-227+ not yet integrated into the app** — Ismail's local `svg2/` folder has real progress well beyond what's wired in (227 SVG files as of 2026-08-27, some pages have alternate variants like `106-surah4.svg`/`106-surah5.svg` and `221-surah10.svg`/`221-surah11.svg` alongside the canonical `106.svg`/`221.svg` — needs a decision on which variant to use per page before integrating those specific ones). See Phase 71.7 for the exact integration pattern to repeat.
- [ ] **JSON polygon files are no longer needed for ayah control, architecture decision 2026-08-26 (76.3i)** — the own-engine renderer already gives full ayah/word control for every page without any external JSON. The `.json` files (currently exist for pages 1-11) are optional bonus data only relevant if tap-to-select is later wanted specifically *within* the real-art "Book" visual layout (76.1g) — not a blocker for anything, and Ismail was told explicitly not to spend further effort collecting more of them.
- [ ] **No colored/ornamental border on the new real-art pages** — this source is monochrome; Ismail hasn't confirmed yet whether that's final or whether a Flutter-drawn decorative frame should be layered on top.

## Phase 14 — Adaptive memorization pace engine (Ismail's request 2026-08-16) — ✅ Sub-phases A + B DONE (commits 4d1d7b5, f1ce946)

Ismail strongly criticized the memorization system as "رص صفحات لا اتقان" (page-stacking, not mastery) and pasted a detailed external design brief demanding a real adaptive "personal coach." Explicitly required a full project analysis before any code — see `C:\Users\ismail\.claude\plans\quirky-gliding-shell.md` for the saved plan. Honest correction delivered to Ismail as part of the plan: the 6-station Ebbinghaus review engine (`memorization_repository.dart`) already IS real mastery tracking, not page-stacking — but several genuine gaps existed and are catalogued below.

- [x] **Real architectural discovery**: `CompletionGoalRepository` (§4.15, خطة الختم) already had exactly the "live ahead/on-track/behind + reschedule" engine the brief asked for, and already supported `content_type: 'quran_memorization'` — it was simply never wired to any UI. `رحلتي` used a separate, simpler, frozen-pace system (`journey_plan`) instead. No new adaptive-planning logic was built from scratch — `JourneyPlanRepository` was rebuilt to ride entirely on the existing engine.
- [x] `MemorizationRepository.nextRecommendedUnit()` — "تكليف اليوم": the first never-started page in Mushaf order, deterministic (same style as `dueToday()`), closing the literal root of the "page-stacking" complaint (previously the student had to browse the whole Mushaf and pick a page manually with zero guidance).
- [x] `رحلتي` dashboard: live متقدم/بالموعد/متأخر badge, "تكليف اليوم" card with a direct "ابدأ الحفظ" deep link into `QuranBrowseScreen` (new optional `highlightUnitId` param), and a non-judgmental "أعِد التخطيط" choice screen when behind (extend at current pace / intensify to the original date) — reuses `CompletionGoalRepository.reschedule()` directly, no new re-planning math written.
- [x] Found-and-fixed in passing: `CompletionGoal.unitLabel` for `quran_memorization` said "آية" (ayah) but the actual counting is per-page — never surfaced before since this code path was dead until now.
- [x] Deliberately simplified vs. the old design: dropped the old "trial week at half pace" softening — the live-recalculation engine doesn't false-flag "behind" on day 1 anyway (daysLeft ≈ totalDays on a fresh plan), so the extra mechanism wasn't needed.
- [x] **Sub-phase B DONE (commit f1ce946)**: `session_time_budget.dart` splits a chosen session length across 5 phases (مراجعة سريعة/حفظ جديد/تكرار/تسميع/فهم) by explicit, documented level-based ratios (labeled honestly as a design heuristic, not a scientific standard). `GuidedSessionScreen` walks through the phases in order, each opening the real existing screen for it (no duplicated logic), ending in a self-rated difficulty (سهلة/مناسبة/صعبة) + optional note, stored as 3 new columns on `daily_session_log` (DB v22→v23, no new table). `رحلتي` shows a non-forced advisory card when 3 consecutive sessions were all hard or all easy, linking directly into the existing "أعِد التخطيط" dialog from Sub-phase A — never a silent automatic pace change. Entry point: a "جلسة موجّهة بالوقت" button atop the existing free-order "جلسة اليوم" checklist, which stays available for whoever prefers it.

## Phase 16 — Two correctness bugs from Ismail's on-device testing (2026-08-16) — ✅ DONE

- [x] **Prayer times displayed in UTC, not local time** (commit 8783aa7) — `adhan_dart` builds every prayer time internally as a UTC `DateTime` (`DateTime.utc(...)`, confirmed by reading the package source directly); 3 separate `_formatTime` implementations (`prayer_times_screen.dart` ×2, `daily_companion_card.dart`) read `.hour`/`.minute` straight off the UTC value with no `.toLocal()`. Fixed all 3. The prayer-selection logic itself (`currentPrayer`/`nextPrayer`) was never affected — Dart `DateTime` comparisons are correct regardless of UTC/local flag, only the *display* was wrong. Re-ran the full 201-assertion `prayer_times_validation_test.dart` suite after the fix — still 100% passing, confirming the underlying astronomical calculation was never the problem.
- [x] **Quran search missed words containing madd marks** (commit 1852857) — e.g. searching "الفقراء" returned nothing despite the word existing. Root-caused by directly inspecting every non-base-letter character actually present in the bundled Tanzil Uthmani text (not guessed): the word is spelled with a combining MADDA ABOVE (U+0653) before the hamza, and `arabic_normalize.dart`'s search-stripping regex only covered basic tashkeel (U+064B-0652), missing U+0653-0655 (madda/hamza-above/hamza-below) and the entire Quranic small-mark/waqf-annotation block (U+06D6-06ED) — both used thousands of times across the real text. Fixed the regex, **plus** a DB v23→v24 data migration recomputing `quran_ayat.text_normalized` for all 6236 ayat, since that column is computed once at import time (not live per search) — existing installs (including Ismail's phone) had the stale under-stripped values baked in and needed the recompute, not just the regex fix.
- [ ] Not yet re-tested on-device by Ismail after either fix.

## Phase 19 — Two blank-screen bugs + Mushaf page header + رحلتي visibility (Ismail's report 2026-08-16) — ✅ DONE (commit 72cde00)

Ismail reported two screens rendering completely blank (app bar title showing, body empty): "القرآن" (browse/mark-memorized) and "جلسة موجّهة" after tapping "ابدأ الجلسة". He also said "رحلتي"/the memorization coach was hard to find, and asked for the reading page's header to show the surah name on top with جزء/page number below, matching real Mushaf page conventions.

- [x] **Found and fixed the likely root cause for the guided-session blank screen**: `_buildPhase()` used a `Spacer()` inside a `Column` set directly as the Scaffold body (no `ListView`/scrollable wrapper) — a known-fragile pattern that can fail under unbounded/degenerate layout constraints. Rewrote it as a `ListView`, which structurally cannot hit that failure mode. Audited every other `Spacer()` usage in the app (`grep`) — all others are safely inside a bounded `Row`, so this was an isolated instance, not systemic.
- [x] **Could not reproduce the القرآن-browse blank screen directly** — added real error-visibility instead of silence: `QuranBrowseScreen` now wraps its load in try/catch and shows a visible error message + retry button, or an empty-state message + retry, instead of rendering nothing. If it recurs, the next screenshot will show the actual cause.
- [x] Fixed a secondary risk in the same area: the "حدّد ما حفظته" flow did `Navigator.pop()` then immediately `Navigator.push()` in the same synchronous callback — deferred the push via `addPostFrameCallback` to avoid same-frame navigation timing issues.
- [x] Quran reading page header redesigned: surah name on top, "الجزء X · الصفحة Y" below, replacing the plain "صفحة N" title — matches real printed Mushaf page header convention. `QuranAyahText`/`ayatForPage` extended with `juzNumber`.
- [x] رحلتي surfaced more visibly inside the القرآن screen: a persistent tappable banner above the reading content, plus a "رحلتي ومدرب الحفظ" entry in the "طريقة عرض المصحف" sheet (previously only reachable via the profile grid).
- [ ] **Not yet re-tested on-device** — fresh APK built and sent for this batch specifically since blank-screen bugs need real confirmation they're actually gone, not just that analyze passes.

## Phase 23 — Audio player: broken YouTube-fallback link + دفتر الفوائد edit/delete/resume-point (Ismail's report 2026-08-16) — ✅ DONE (commit 0fe0a29)

Ismail reported (with screenshots showing a "This video is unavailable — Error code: 152" embed failure) that "افتح هذه الحلقة في يوتيوب" did nothing when tapped, and asked for the ability to edit/delete دفتر الفوائد notes plus a manual "where did I stop" field as a fallback for when the embedded player breaks.

- [x] Root cause found: `_currentVideoId` was only ever set reactively from the player's metadata stream, which never fires when a video fails to embed — so the fallback link (and the reflections filter) had no video id on exactly the broken videos. Now set synchronously whenever a video is cued (`_setCurrentVideo`), independent of embed success.
- [x] `AudioLibraryRepository.updateReflection()`/`deleteReflection()` — UI edit (dialog) and delete (confirm) icons added to each note
- [x] Optional `resume_note` free-text field (DB v28→v29, additive column) saved alongside each reflection — a manual position record (link or "دقيقة 15") that survives a broken embed, shown on the note card
- [x] `flutter analyze`/`flutter test` clean
- [ ] Not yet tested on-device.
- [ ] **Not built this round, flagged as a much larger follow-up**: Ismail shared his own personal tracking spreadsheet (`life_project.xlsx`, 19 sheets — Quran memorization, tafsir listening, Wasitiyyah, Zad al-Ma'ad, daily wird, Arba'in, takbir, congregation prayer, tasbih/istighfar, plus secular sheets: sleep, exercise, programming, work/freelance, social media, monthly review) as the target level of detail: per-entry link/status/start-end datetime/rich benefit notes/weekly-report field/hours-total, applied uniformly across every content pillar. This is a genuinely large structural expansion (a generalized daily-log-with-weekly-report pattern reused across every existing pillar, not just audio) — architecturally fits the local-SQLite model fine, no blocker, but is a multi-session undertaking on its own, not a quick add-on. Needs explicit prioritization from Ismail before starting, same as the "prayer OS" scoping in §4.25.

## Phase 28 — Real hifz coaching methodology: سبق/سبقي/منزل, "حبة حبة" (Ismail's request 2026-08-16) — 🔄 IN PROGRESS

Ismail: "ركّز وابحث كيف تكون مدرب شخصي لتطوير المستخدم في القرآن والتعلم، ابحث اونلاين اولًا وبعدين تعال" — real research (`WebSearch`/`WebFetch`) before any code. Found the traditional Sabaq-Sabqi-Manzil hifz-teaching system (real institute methodology, not a blog) with concrete numbers: real daily new-memorization pace by level, the "must be flawless before advancing" mastery rule, the 15-day سبقي window, and the weekly-proportional منزل rotation. Full findings in `QURAN_COMPANION_ROADMAP.md` §4.31. Ismail confirmed: "نعم وسنطور اكثر حبه حبه" (yes, and we'll develop it further step by step) — building incrementally, not all at once.

- [x] Grain 1 (commit 1913ec3): `MemorizationUnit.hifzCategory()` — pure function categorizing a page as سبق/سبقي/منزل by age since memorized, unit-tested (`test/hifz_category_test.dart`, 4 cases). ## Phase 35 — AR Qibla bearing bug fix: gimbal lock + reversed heading (Ismail's on-device report 2026-08-16)

Ismail tested Phase 33's changes and reported two concrete regressions: "البوصلة تحتاج ان ارفع الكامرة الى الاعلى" (needed to raise the camera up for it to settle) and "انعكست" (the direction reversed). He also pasted a long, well-researched multi-layer Qibla architecture proposal (Location Engine → Qibla Calculation → Orientation Engine → Validation Engine → Qibla UI) and asked me to research online and get stronger on this specifically, since he considers it the single hardest part of the app.

**Root cause, found by working the actual rotation math rather than guessing**: `flutter_rotation_sensor`'s default `azimuth` tracks the compass bearing of the phone's **top edge** (its body Y-axis) — correct for the flat-held compass dial, but wrong for a phone held vertically like a camera. Held that way, the top-edge axis points mostly toward the sky, so its horizontal component shrinks toward zero — a near-gimbal-lock condition where tiny sensor noise swings the reported azimuth wildly, which only settles once the phone tilts back toward flat (exactly the "raise the camera" symptom). Using the wrong body axis for "which way the lens points" also explains the reversed reading.

- [x] **Fix**: `QiblaArView` now computes its own bearing directly from the orientation event's rotation matrix, using the body **-Z axis** (the direction the back camera actually looks) via `atan2(-m.c, -m.f)`, instead of the default Y-axis-based azimuth. Verified algebraically before shipping: reproduced the package's own documented default-azimuth formula from its matrix math to confirm the derivation method is sound, then re-derived for the camera-facing axis and sanity-checked it against two independent hand-worked cases (camera pointing north → 0°, camera pointing east → 90°) — not a blind guess. Held vertically, the back-camera axis is naturally close to horizontal, so it avoids the gimbal-lock zone entirely instead of requiring the user to compensate.
- [x] The compass dial tab (`_CompassView`, meant to be used flat) correctly keeps using the default Y-axis azimuth — the bug was AR-view-specific, not present in the dial.
- [x] `QiblaArView` now runs its own local orientation subscription again (reverted the earlier "share one fused heading between both tabs" design from Phase 33 — that sharing was itself part of the problem, since the two views genuinely need different axis conventions, not just different smoothing).
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean, debug APK built.
- [ ] Not yet confirmed fixed on-device by Ismail — I have no way to test the actual sensor behavior myself.

**Researched but deliberately not built this round — magnetic vs. true north declination correction**: Ismail's pasted proposal correctly flags that `adhan_dart`'s Qibla bearing is computed from pure geography (a **true-north**-relative great-circle bearing), while the device's compass heading is **magnetic-north**-relative — a real, currently-uncorrected gap on Android specifically (this app's compass tab already uses `flutter_compass`, which on iOS typically already returns `CLHeading.trueHeading`, but Android has no equivalent built-in correction). `flutter_rotation_sensor`'s own `ReferenceFrame.trueNorth` option **silently falls back to magneticNorth on Android** (confirmed by reading its own source doc comments), so it wouldn't actually fix anything on the platform Ismail is testing. The one pure-Dart declination package found (`geomag` on pub.dev) bundles **WMM-2015v2 coefficients from 2018** — over a decade stale by 2026, which could introduce its own new error rather than reliably fixing the existing one. Decided not to bolt on an unverified, stale-data package for an unverifiable (no device) fix; flagged as a real follow-up needing either fresh WMM/IGRF coefficients or a small native platform-channel call to Android's own `GeomagneticField` class, not built today.

**Confirmed already correct, in response to Ismail's proposal's core worry**: the Qibla bearing itself is calculated purely from location/geography (`adhan_dart`'s `Qibla.qibla()`, a great-circle bearing from lat/lng to the Kaaba) — the compass is only ever used to compare the device's *current* heading against that pre-computed target, never to determine the target itself. This already matches the "don't calculate Qibla from the compass" principle his proposal opens with.

## Phase 40 — Adhkar reading: swipe direction feedback (Ismail's on-device report 2026-08-16)

Ismail tested Phase 37's continuous reading flow: "يعمل جيدًا ولكن السحب... من اليسار الى اليمين أفضل". Clarified via `AskUserQuestion` before touching anything (axis change is exactly the kind of gesture decision this session already decided not to guess blind, per Phase 32's swipe-direction risk note): confirmed he wants horizontal swipe instead of vertical, right-to-left drag = next dua (matching Arabic reading direction).

- [x] Rebuilt `adhkar_category_screen.dart`'s navigation from vertical scroll-overscroll detection to a horizontal `PageView`. Confirmed the app is globally locked to `TextDirection.rtl` (`main.dart`'s `MaterialApp.builder` wraps everything in `Directionality(textDirection: TextDirection.rtl)`), so Flutter resolves the `PageView`'s page order against that automatically — no manual sign-flipping needed for "right-to-left = forward."
- [x] This also **simplified** the implementation, not just changed it: the original vertical design needed custom overscroll-based navigation specifically to avoid a same-axis conflict between page-swiping and read-scrolling (both vertical). With paging now horizontal and reading-scroll vertical, those are independent axes — each dua page is back to being a plain `SingleChildScrollView`, no custom gesture-detection code needed at all. `_DuaPage` and `_CompletionPage` both got smaller as a result.
- [x] Session-position persistence now driven by `PageView.onPageChanged` instead of manual `_goNext`/`_goPrevious` state updates — same `AdhkarRepository.saveSessionPosition` call, just wired to `PageController`'s own page-change event.
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` 215/216 (same pre-existing unrelated failure).
- [x] **Reversed again same day** per Ismail's immediate follow-up after trying it on-device: "اجعله يعمل من اليسار الى اليمين لاتمام والعكس للرجوع" — left-to-right = next, right-to-left = back (the opposite of what §Phase 40 above had just built). Fixed by wrapping only the `PageView` itself in a local `Directionality(textDirection: TextDirection.ltr)` override — flips which physical drag direction Flutter treats as "forward" for that one widget without touching the rest of the RTL app — and re-wrapping each page's actual content in `Directionality(textDirection: TextDirection.rtl)` so the Arabic text itself is unaffected by the outer override.
- [x] **Reverted back to right-to-left = next (final)** — Ismail tried the left-to-right build on-device and reported it as reversed again: "الحركه معكوسه اعكسها... اعكس الحركه المطبقه في الانتقال الى الدعاء التالى", quoting back the previous send-caption to mean the just-shipped behavior itself was wrong. Removed the `Directionality(ltr)` override around the `PageView` (and the now-redundant inner `Directionality(rtl)` wrapper around each page) entirely — right-to-left = next is simply the app's ambient RTL default with zero extra code, which is what this settles on. Hint text flipped back to "اسحب لليسار للمتابعة". `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` 215/216 (same pre-existing unrelated failure).
- [ ] Not yet confirmed on-device — fourth iteration of this gesture, needs real-finger verification again. This should be the last one baring further feedback: it matches the app's natural RTL default rather than fighting it with an override.

## Phase 41 — Book notebook: دفتر الفوائد + سجل التطبيق for PDF books (Ismail's request 2026-08-16)

Ismail attached a real 8,468-page scanned PDF ("نضرة النعيم في مكارم أخلاق الرسول الكريم ﷺ") and asked for it to be "embodied" in the app — read, applied, with notes recorded — as a curriculum. Investigated directly rather than assuming: confirmed via `pypdf` (page count + sampled text extraction across pages 1, 30, 50, 80, 120, 200, 500, 1000 — all `len=0`, JBIG2 image encoding, `jbig2dec binary is not available` on image inspection) that this is a **scanned, non-OCR'd PDF with zero extractable text anywhere sampled** — technically no way to auto-generate quiz questions or curated lesson text from it in this environment. Reported this honestly before building anything, per this session's established sourcing-integrity discipline.

Fallback: "مكتبتي" (personal library)'s local `file_picker` path has no such size limit.
- [x] Built the genuinely new piece — a "دفتر الفوائد" (reading reflections) + "سجل التطبيق" (real-life application log) for any PDF book opened via `BookViewerScreen`, explicitly mirroring the audio library's existing `audio_reflection_log` UX per Ismail's direct instruction ("اجعل هذي الخاصية نفس المكتبة الصوتية"): same prompt-chip pattern, same add/edit/delete flow, same card layout — adapted only where the domain genuinely differs (page number instead of a manual resume-note, since books already have a robust auto-saved page bookmark that audio's resume-note was compensating for; a separate application-log section since "what did you apply today" is a different kind of entry than a reading note).
- [x] DB migration v32→v33: `book_reflection_log(id, book_key, page, reflection_text, created_date)` + `book_application_log(id, book_key, application_text, created_date)`, both indexed on `book_key` — the same stable identifier `BookViewerScreen`/`BookBookmark` already use, so this works identically whether the book came from the Telegram feed or the personal library.

## Phase 42 — Adhkar content taxonomy: dhikr/dua/occasion sections (Ismail's request 2026-08-16)

Ismail pasted a large external "Content Ontology" proposal (8 content types, a live LOCATION/ACTIVITY trigger engine, a full automated import pipeline, hadith-authenticity grading). Evaluated it directly rather than either rejecting or blindly implementing it: the core diagnosis was accurate (`adhkar_categories` had zero classification column — "أذكار الصباح والمساء" and "دعاء السفر" were structurally identical rows, distinguished only by title text), but the trigger engine needs sensor/background-location infrastructure this app has none of, an automated import pipeline is enterprise-content-team tooling for a single already-imported 134-category book, and the authenticity/hadith-grading field directly conflicts with Ismail's own earlier explicit, repeated safety instruction from the ruqyah episode this session ("لا تدخل أي شيء يودي إلى مشاكل"). Presented this scoped-down assessment; Ismail explicitly chose "النسخة الكاملة من الاقتراح" (the full version) knowing the exclusions. Used `EnterPlanMode` given the scale (schema change + UI redesign + real risk of breaking 3 existing features that hardcode a category title string) — spawned an Explore agent that read the actual, complete 134-category title list from `assets/adhkar/hisn_almuslim.json` and traced every functional reference to `adhkar_categories`/title strings across the codebase before any code was written.

- [x] **Manually classified all 134 real category titles** (not a blind prefix heuristic — several titles like "التشهد" or "الاستغفار والتوبة" carry no obvious دعاء/ذكر prefix and needed actual judgment) into `content_type` (`dhikr`/`dua`/`other`) and, for the real occasion groupings that already exist in the book, `occasion` (`travel`/`funeral`/`hajj`/`food`). This classification pass *is* the "human review" step the external proposal's own pipeline called for — done directly rather than deferred to tooling.
- [x] **Verified zero typos before trusting it**: wrote a script cross-checking every title string embedded in the migration against the actual JSON's 134 titles — 135/135 title-strings matched exactly, zero silently-dead `WHERE title = ?` clauses.
- [x] DB migration v33→v34 (`_createV34Tables`): `ALTER TABLE adhkar_categories ADD COLUMN content_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'dhikr'` + `ADD COLUMN occasion TEXT`, then batched `UPDATE ... WHERE title = ?` statements applying the classification (86 dua, 12 other, the remaining 19 non-core categories default to dhikr; 37 occasion tags across the 4 real groups). **Purely additive — no category is renamed or deleted**, which was the deliberate design choice protecting the 3 places that hardcode the exact title "أذكار الصباح والمساء" (`adhkar_category_screen.dart`'s streak constant, `adhkar_journey.dart`'s journey suggestions consumed by `daily_journey_card.dart`, `wird_repository.dart`'s raw SQL title lookup feeding `DailyCompanionCard`'s "الأذكار" chip) — verified via count-sanity-check that all 134 rows are accounted for with no accidental overlap between the dua/other classification lists.
- [x] `AdhkarCategory`/`AdhkarRepository.allCategories()` extended with `contentType`/`occasion` fields — purely additive to the model, no existing method touched.
- [x] Rebuilt `adhkar_screen.dart` from the old binary core/rest split into 5 real sections matching Ismail's own sketch (point 15 of his message): "عبادتك اليوم" (unchanged `is_daily_core` set), "أذكارك", "أدعية", "مناسبات وأعمال" (sub-grouped by occasion, occasion take precedence over type for display grouping — matches the proposal's "a collection groups by context, not just type" principle), "أخرى" for the dozen informational/procedural entries that are neither dhikr nor dua by the term's actual meaning.
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` 215/216 (same pre-existing unrelated failure).
- [ ] Not yet confirmed on-device — in particular, worth Ismail double-checking that the streak/journey-card/wird-chip features (which depend on the untouched title string, not on anything changed this phase) still behave exactly as before.

## Phase 44 — "الدماغ الذي يربط طالب العلم", Batch 1: generalized spaced-review engine (Ismail's request 2026-08-16)

Ismail reviewed the full feature map ([خارطة طالب العلم](artifact)) and sent a 20-point analysis diagnosing the real gap: rich content across 8+ pillars but no "brain" connecting them — the app never tells the student what to review, what's overdue, or what's next. Explicit anti-AI-judgment principle from earlier this session (ruqyah episode) reaffirmed and applied: no automated grading of religious content, self-declared ratings only where needed. Scoped via `AskUserQuestion` into 2 decisions: (1) prerequisites stay **advisory only, no hard lock** — preserves the app's documented "companion not manager" principle in `curriculum_levels.dart`; (2) build in **5 sequential batches**, not one giant pass. Full research (3 parallel Explore agents) + the full 5-batch plan is in `EnterPlanMode`'s saved plan file, approved before any code.

**Batch 1 — generalized spaced review** ("مراجعتك اليوم"), because it reuses the existing 6-station Quran engine's *logic* (verified pure/table-agnostic by reading `MemorizationRepository.recordReview` line-by-line) without touching Quran's own table/data — lowest risk, unlocks the most value first.

- [x] DB migration v34→v35: `knowledge_review_progress(item_type, item_id, status, station, last_review_date, next_review_date, consecutive_good_count)` — a **new, separate** table from `memorization_progress`, so Quran memorization is completely untouched.
- [x] `lib/services/spaced_repetition_engine.dart` — the exact 3-branch station logic (`[1,3,7,14,30,60]` days; needsReview→station 2 fixed; good→same station, rescheduled; excellent→station+1 or `established` at the top) extracted as pure functions with zero DB access, so it can be shared between Quran (unchanged) and the new generalized table.
- [x] `KnowledgeReviewRepository`: `startReviewing`/`recordReview`/`dueToday`/`dueTodayAll`, generalized over `item_type ∈ {'hadith','wasitiyyah'}`.
- [x] `HadithRepository.markMemorized`/`WasitiyyahRepository.markMemorized` now also call `startReviewing` (additive — the existing flat "memorized" flag every other screen depends on is untouched).
- [x] `hadith_quiz_screen.dart`/`wasitiyyah_quiz_screen.dart`'s existing self-report buttons ("كنت أذكره"/"أحتاج مراجعة") now also call `recordReview` — these quizzes already only sample already-memorized items, so this was the natural, single wiring point (confirmed via grep: `markMemorized` is called from 3 separate screens each, so hooking the repository method itself, not each call site, was the only way to not miss one).
- [x] New screen `knowledge_review_screen.dart` ("مراجعتك اليوم") — cross-pillar due list (Quran/Hadith/Wasitiyyah), tapping a section opens that pillar's existing review/quiz screen rather than a new per-item UI.
- [x] New home-screen entry card `KnowledgeReviewEntryCard` — hides itself entirely when nothing is due (never shows a permanent "0").
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` 215/216 (same pre-existing unrelated failure).

**Remaining batches (2-5), not started yet**: اختبار تحديد المستوى (placement self-assessment) → المسار التعليمي + الإرشادات (advisory prerequisites, no lock) → المدرّب المتكيّف مع الوقت (generalize the existing guided-session engine across pillars) → لوحة طالب العلم (home dashboard tying it together). Full detail for each in the approved plan file.

## Phase 45 — التحفيظ الصوتي: Quran audio provider + memorization playback engine (Ismail's request 2026-08-16, P0)

Ismail sent a reference screenshot of a "التحفيظ" (recitation-practice) screen — reciter picker, ayah range, range-repeat/single-ayah-repeat/silence-length settings — and asked to add it to Quran memorization. Investigated first rather than assuming: confirmed via direct code inspection that the app has **zero per-ayah recitation audio source** at all (an existing comment in the codebase says so explicitly). Ismail then set this as **P0**, ahead of finishing the remaining "الدماغ" batches, and sent a precise technical spec: separate the audio source from the playback engine completely (`AudioProvider` abstraction), research real licenses before downloading anything, selective per-surah download only (never bundle the whole Quran), no AI recitation-correction yet.

**Licensing research actually done** (`WebFetch`, not assumed): confirmed EveryAyah.com serves real per-ayah files (`SSSAAA.mp3`) across 70 reciter folders, but its own referenced license page (`versebyversequran.com/site/license`) is now a dead link (404) — reported honestly. Found and read the official Quran Foundation Content API's live Developer Terms instead: audio is streaming-only, content can't be cached >7 days without periodic re-sync, personal non-commercial use is fine, commercial redistribution needs a separate license. Ismail registered a real Quran Foundation app and pasted live `client_id`/`client_secret` mid-session — before wiring anything, checked the API's own authentication docs, which state explicitly **"Do not call the token endpoint from browser or mobile code"** (the secret would be extractable from a distributed APK). Flagged this to Ismail directly; decided with him to keep `QuranFoundationProvider` in the codebase but fully inert (no network calls, `isConfigured` hardcoded false) until a server-side token proxy exists — building that server contradicts the app's local-first design and is out of scope for this pass. `EveryAyahProvider` is what actually runs today, needing zero setup.

- [x] `lib/services/quran_audio/` — `QuranAudioProvider` abstract interface (`reciters()`, `ayahAudioUrl()`, `isConfigured`), `EveryAyahProvider` (17 curated reciters, one bitrate each, verified URL pattern `https://everyayah.com/data/{folder}/{surah:03}{ayah:03}.mp3`), `QuranFoundationProvider` (real credentials obtained but deliberately inert — see doc comment), `QuranAudioProviderRegistry` (tries providers in priority order, silent fallback).
- [x] `QuranAudioDownloadService` — mirrors the existing `DownloadedFileService` pattern exactly (`getApplicationDocumentsDirectory()` + `quran_audio/{reciterId}/` subfolder, cache-first `File.exists()` check). No separate DB table for download state — checks the filesystem directly, same "can't drift out of sync" philosophy as `CurriculumRepository`. `downloadRange`/`downloadedAyahCount`/`totalSizeBytes`/`deleteRange` walk (surah, ayah) pairs correctly across surah boundaries (confirmed via `MemorizationUnit`'s doc comment that Mushaf pages don't always align to surah boundaries).
- [x] `QuranAudioEngine` (`lib/services/quran_audio_engine.dart`) — built on `audioplayers` (existing dependency, same pattern as `book_screen.dart`'s `_VoicePlayButton`). Implements Ismail's exact method list: `playAyah`/`playRange`/`repeatAyah`/`repeatRange`/`setPauseBetweenAyat`/`setRepeatCount`/`playListenAndRepeatSession`/`resumeSession`. Prefers a locally downloaded file over streaming. Silence length in `playListenAndRepeatSession` is the ayah's own playback duration × a configurable multiplier (matches the screenshot's "طول السكتة، بقدر الآية" — a longer ayah gets a longer pause), not a fixed number of seconds.
- [x] `TahfeezSessionPrefs` — persists the active session's config + current position (`SharedPreferences`) after every ayah, so `resumeSession()` survives the app being closed mid-session.
- [x] `tahfeez_session_setup_screen.dart` + `tahfeez_playback_screen.dart` — new screens matching the reference screenshot's fields (own visual style, not a copy of its look). Wired into an entry point that already existed as a disabled placeholder: `quran_reading_screen.dart`'s display-options sheet had a greyed-out "الصوتيات" row calling `_notAvailable(...)` — replaced with a real, working "التحفيظ الصوتي" entry.
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean, `flutter test` 215/216 (same pre-existing unrelated failure).
- [ ] Not yet confirmed on-device — first real test: pick a reciter, play a short range, confirm streaming works with zero prior setup, then download that range and confirm playback switches to the local file.
- [ ] Deferred, per Ismail's own instructions: AI recitation correction/analysis, student voice recording, wiring `QuranFoundationProvider` for real (needs a server-side token proxy first).
- [x] **Follow-up same day**: added live ayah-text display to `tahfeez_playback_screen.dart` — Ismail asked for the currently-playing/repeating ayah to be visually highlighted while listening. Implemented by fetching the actual Uthmani text for the current (surah, ayah) directly from `quran_ayat` (cached per ayah so repeats don't re-query) and showing it prominently in a highlighted card — simpler and more directly useful than trying to sync highlight state into the separate full-page Mushaf reading screen. `flutter analyze`/`test` re-verified clean.

Separately, he directly called out the دليل المسلم الجديد stick-figure illustrations as unacceptably primitive ("رسومات بداية من العصر الحجري") and asked to use Blender + ready-made assets rather than building from scratch.

**Real experimentation done, not just discussion**: confirmed Blender is actually installed (`C:\Program Files\Blender Foundation`, versions 4.4 and 5.1) and invocable headlessly from this terminal (`blender.exe --background --python script.py`) — ran an actual test render of a CC0-licensed rigged human model (`github.com/UMRAM-Bilkent/supine-human-model`) in ~2 seconds, proving the pipeline works technically. But the actual rendered output (a tiny, faceless, badly-framed low-poly test dummy) confirmed the real risk flagged earlier: a generic test asset with no art direction doesn't produce usable illustration quality. Ismail agreed to pause Blender/MPFB after seeing this real evidence, rather than continuing to invest based on discussion alone.

Also test-drove a "download ready-made" 2D alternative (Humaaans, a popular CC0-claimed illustration library) — cloned the actual GitHub mirror and found a real discrepancy: the official site claims CC0, but the package's own bundled `LICENSE.md` is CC BY 4.0 (attribution required), and the set has no bowing/prostrating poses at all (only standing/sitting) — rejected for the guide's prayer-position needs specifically, documented as a worked example in the new asset-tracking doc.

Verified Rive and Lottie's actual `pub.dev` license/offline-capability facts directly rather than assuming. **Decision: do not adopt Rive/Lottie/Blender yet** — every current motion need is already served by native Flutter, just inconsistently.
- [x] **`lib/theme/motion.dart`** (`AppMotion`) — 4 shared duration tiers (`fast`/`normal`/`premium`/`ambient`) and 3 named curves, derived from what the existing 19 call sites already converged toward (not invented numbers) — a vocabulary for new work, not a rewrite of the 19 existing sites.
- [x] **`MOTION_BIBLE.md`** — documents the tiers + the 5-template-screen plan status.
- [x] **Redrew all 4 `دليل المسلم الجديد` illustrations** (`lib/widgets/guide_diagram.dart`) from bare stick-figure outlines to filled, proportioned, labeled figures: wudu (face/arms/head-wipe/feet wash zones labeled, matching the reference screenshot's labeled-diagram spirit without copying it), ghusl (full standing figure with cascading water), istinja (kept deliberately non-figurative per the original design call, but with a proper gradient fill), salah (three labeled postures: قيام/ركوع/سجود, filled bodies instead of thin stick lines). Same `CustomPainter`-only approach as before (no bundled image/SVG asset, avoiding any licensing question entirely) — just drawn with real effort this time.

## Phase 49 — Adhkar memorization (text-only) + audio licensing research (Ismail's request 2026-08-16)

Ismail asked for the same تحفيظ (listen/pause/repeat) treatment applied to Adhkar/Dua, and pasted an external research message proposing real audio sources. Investigated the two most promising leads directly (`WebFetch`, not trusted at face value): `abdurrahman.org`'s Hisn al-Muslim MP3s state only *"posted with implicit/explicit permission from content owners"* — no explicit redistribution grant. **Conclusion: no fully-licensed, complete 298-item Adhkar audio collection currently exists that this app can legally bundle.** Full audit in new `ADHKAR_AUDIO_SOURCES.md`, including a genuinely promising unresolved lead (House of Islam Developer API, explicitly covers Dhikr/Dua content, "personal and commercial use alike," but unconfirmed whether audio is included — needs Ismail to register an API key himself to find out, same boundary as Quran Foundation).

Rather than blocking the whole feature on that licensing question, separated the two halves architecturally per Ismail's own correction: the **memorization engine** (text, spaced repetition, recall testing) doesn't need audio at all and ships today; the **audio layer** stays a separate, optional, pluggable enhancement for whenever licensed content exists — nothing about the audio architecture is being removed or deprioritized, just correctly sequenced.

Idempotent (a snackbar says so if already added).

## Phase 9 — Qibla direction + prayer-times engine (Ismail's request 2026-08-15) — ✅ DONE 2026-08-15 (commit 4826f53)

Architecture Ismail specified and Claude agreed to (2026-08-15): `Location Service → Astronomical Calculation Engine → Prayer Jurisprudence Configuration → Validation Engine → Prayer Schedule → Notifications`, fully offline after an initial GPS fix. Used `adhan_dart` (MIT-licensed, published/audited astronomical formulas) as the verified calculation core, not hand-derived formulas.

- [x] Location service (GPS via `geolocator` + cached-last-fix + manual-entry fallback) — `lib/services/location_service.dart`
- [x] Prayer Calculation Engine wrapping `adhan_dart` — 13 calculation methods + madhab (Asr timing) configurable and persisted — `lib/repositories/prayer_times_repository.dart`
- [x] Independent Validation Engine — `test/prayer_times_validation_test.dart`, 201 assertions: chronological ordering across 8 real cities × 5 dates × 4 methods, Dhuhr cross-checked against solar noon computed independently via equation-of-time (not the library's own math). Genuinely caught a wrong assumption mid-development (Dhuhr isn't bit-identical across methods — some apply a small conventional offset) and the test was corrected, not the library.
- [x] Qibla compass — bearing via `adhan_dart`'s verified great-circle formula, heading via `flutter_compass` (magnetometer+accelerometer fusion), confidence indicator derived from the sensor's own reported accuracy (never claims precision it doesn't have), calibration prompt at low confidence
- [x] Home screen integration — ✅ DONE 2026-08-15 (commit 0dd0c5b). "رفيقك اليوم" combined card (`widgets/daily_companion_card.dart`) shows next prayer + today's Quran/adhkar status + a Qibla quick-link together, reusing existing repositories rather than duplicating status logic. Silently hides if location isn't available; standalone screens/buttons stay too.
- [x] High-latitude rule — ✅ DONE 2026-08-15 (commit d48521c). Exposed as a setting in PrayerTimesScreen (auto/middleOfTheNight/seventhOfTheNight/twilightAngle), defaults to `adhan_dart`'s own `HighLatitudeRule.recommended()`.

## Phase 13 — كتب صوتية من اليوتيوب: audio lecture library (Ismail's request 2026-08-16) — ✅ DONE 2026-08-16 (commit 3d0f050)

Ismail sent 3 YouTube links asking to add them as an in-app "audio books from YouTube" section, first explicitly checking whether this is allowed. Confirmed: streaming through YouTube's own official embedded player (`youtube_player_iframe`, wraps the IFrame Player API) is fully compliant and standard practice — the app never downloads/rehosts the actual media, only metadata (video/playlist IDs). Ismail separately asked whether downloading audio to the device is possible — confirmed no, that would require stream-extraction (yt-dlp-style), which violates YouTube's ToS and the uploader's copyright; declined to build it, same boundary as the earlier Nabulsi-audio decision.

- [x] Auto-resume: `AudioPlayerScreen` tracks playback position every 15s and on dispose, reopening a series cues the exact video+timestamp last left off at — Ismail's explicit ask ("يسجل أين توقف")
- [x] "دفتر الفوائد" reflection notes per episode, with 4 example prompts as tap-to-insert chips to encourage writing (Ismail's explicit ask for "امثله كي يتشجع للكتابه") — wired into `achievement_milestones` at 1/10/50 saved reflections, per the standing every-pillar-gets-certificates rule
- [x] Self-service "أضف سلسلة صوتية" — Ismail asked whether new series could be added going forward beyond what he'd sent; besides the trivial answer (send more links, they get added to the const list), built an in-app add-your-own-link screen (paste any YouTube video/playlist URL + title) so he isn't blocked waiting on a future session, matching the existing "مكتبتي" personal-library pattern (self-added, no curation/review, clearly separated in the UI from the 3 app-curated series)
- [x] **Tested on-device by Ismail — surfaced two real problems, both fixed 2026-08-16 (commit 22ad834)**: (1) some individual videos fail to embed ("Error code: 152" — a video-side embedding restriction the app cannot override without stream-extraction, which stays off-limits; mitigated with a per-episode "افتح هذه الحلقة في يوتيوب" fallback). (2) the flat single-player screen had no way to browse other episodes in a series and jump around — rebuilt into a real قسم→حلقات (series→episodes) structure: `AudioPlayerScreen` reads the actual ordered episode-video-ID list straight from YouTube's own embedded player (`controller.playlist`, no Data API key needed), shows a tappable "الحلقات" list with السابقة/التالية navigation, and `AudioLibraryRepository.reflectionsFor` now filters "دفتر الفوائد" per episode (`video_id`) instead of mixing every note from the whole series together.

Full round trip confirmed: `file_id` → `getFile` → real download URL → `200 OK` with byte-exact `Content-Length` matching the uploaded 13MB test file (a PDF of "الأذكار للنووي" forwarded from the channel to the bot's DM).

This closes Ismail's "what could uploading PDFs/announcements do for the app" question from earlier in the session — the feature already exists in the Dart code, just needs a live relay.
- [x] Confirmed via `CLAUDE.md` that this was modeled after a sibling project (DawahReportApp) but is explicitly meant to use its **own** bot/sheet/token — Ismail reconfirmed directly ("لا تخلطه مع هاذا، هاذا عمل جديد"), so no code or credentials were read from that other project.
- [x] Wrote `telegram_relay/Code.gs` from scratch against the existing `BookContentFeed` JSON contract (not copied from anywhere): admin-only message parsing (`كتاب:`/`بانر:`/`ملف:`/`سؤال:...جواب:`/plain-text-as-announcement prefixes, filtered to Ismail's own Telegram user id `749369573`, confirmed live this session), a `pollTelegram` time-driven-trigger ingestion function logging into a Sheet, and a `doGet` that compiles the Sheet into the feed JSON.
- [x] `telegram_relay/DEPLOY.md` — step-by-step deployment instructions (new Sheet → Apps Script → Script Properties for `BOT_TOKEN`/`ADMIN_ID` → `setup` → trigger → Web app deploy → paste `/exec` URL into `app_config.dart`'s `bookContentApiUrl`).
- [x] **Known, disclosed security tradeoff**: resolved file download URLs embed the raw bot token (`.../file/bot<TOKEN>/...`) — a genuine Google Apps Script platform limitation (no clean binary-proxy or redirect mechanism available for non-browser HTTP clients), same tradeoff the original dormant `ContentItem.url`/`BookEntry.url` model comments already accepted ("direct Telegram file URL"). Low practical severity: this bot has no capability beyond posting/reading its own chat/channel, and the token is revocable instantly via `@BotFather`'s `/revoke`.

Verified directly: `curl` against the `/exec` URL now returns `{"announcement":"","banner":null,"books":[],"items":[],"history":[],"faq":[]}` — the exact empty-feed shape `BookContentFeed.fromJson` expects. `app_config.dart`'s `bookContentApiUrl` updated with the real URL.
- [x] **Full round trip confirmed live 2026-08-17**: Ismail sent a real "كتاب: تجربة" message with a PDF attached to the bot's private chat, ran `pollTelegram` manually, and the feed correctly returned `books: [{title: "تجربة", url: "...documents/file_1.pdf", ...}]` — verified the resolved download URL itself with `curl -I` (`200 OK`, exact byte-size match). The pipeline (Telegram message → Apps Script parse → Sheet → JSON feed → real file URL) works end-to-end. Two learning-curve false starts along the way, both self-corrected: (1) sending "كتاب: تجربة" as plain text with no attachment — correctly fell through to the `announcement` branch, confirming the fileId-gating logic works as designed; (2) accidentally pasting the JSON example text itself as a message. ## Phase 51 — Daily Adhkar window: real prayer-time accuracy (Ismail's request 2026-08-17)

Ismail asked to work on "طريقة الأذكار اليومية وكيف عرضها" (the daily Adhkar method and how it's displayed) — clarified via `AskUserQuestion` + follow-up correction that this meant improving the existing daily-suggestion display, not audio. `DailyJourneyCard`'s time-of-day suggestion (`dayWindowFor`) used fixed clock hours (6am/4pm etc.) as a rough proxy for "morning"/"evening" adhkar — inaccurate, since real أذكار الصباح/المساء windows are traditionally anchored to Fajr/Asr, which shift by season and location, and this app already computes those accurately app-wide via `adhan_dart` (`PrayerTimesRepository`, already used by `DailyCompanionCard` on the same home screen).

- [x] `lib/data/adhkar_journey.dart`: added `dayWindowFromPrayerTimes(now, PrayerTimes)` — Fajr→Dhuhr is "morning" (أذكار الصباح), Dhuhr→Asr is "afternoon" (استغفار), Asr→Isha is "evening" (أذكار المساء), before-Fajr/after-Isha is "night". The one window with no prayer-time anchor (`earlyMorning`/"استيقاظ", since waking up isn't a prayer time) keeps a fixed 3am lower bound but now uses the real Fajr time as its upper bound instead of a fixed 6am. Old `dayWindowFor(DateTime)` (fixed-clock) kept unchanged as the fallback. New `journeySuggestionFromPrayerTimes()` reuses the same suggestion table.
- [x] `DailyJourneyCard` now fetches location + prayer times (same `LocationService`/`PrayerTimesRepository` pattern as `DailyCompanionCard`, right above it on the home screen) and prefers the accurate window; silently falls back to the old fixed-clock suggestion if location isn't available or the calculation throws — never regresses a device without a location fix.
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean project-wide (same 6 pre-existing unrelated infos).
- [ ] Not yet confirmed on-device.

**Audio topic (Ismail's parallel "اشتغل كله" request) — status, not new work needed**: re-examined after Phase 50's proven pipeline and realized the generic `ملف:` item type already built there (matches ANY attached file, not just PDFs) already covers Adhkar-audio delivery with zero new code — an mp3 sent as `ملف: <title>` would already appear in the feed's `items[]` with a working URL. The two genuinely remaining pieces are non-code: (1) Ismail actually recording/sourcing real Adhkar audio (his own action — still nothing licensed found per `ADHKAR_AUDIO_SOURCES.md`), and (2) building the dedicated listen/pause/repeat/next playback UI, deliberately still deferred until real audio content exists to test against (same principle stated in `ADHKAR_AUDIO_SOURCES.md` — building UI against zero real audio would be shipping something unverifiable).

## Phase 58 — Qibla: real sun-based method, no compass dependency (2026-08-17)

The Qibla screen's 4th tab ("الشمس والقمر") had sat as an honest `_ComingSoonView` placeholder since Phase 127-135's compass-strengthening work — its own text said explicitly "لم نضفه بعد حتى نتأكد من دقته قبل عرضه" (not added until accuracy is confirmed). Ismail's mid-turn request ("اريد اكثر جودة في العالم... من البوصلة الى التزيز الى الاكتشاف عن طريق الشمس") asked for exactly this gap to be closed.

- [x] **`lib/services/solar_position.dart`** (new) — real-time solar altitude/azimuth. Reuses `adhan_dart`'s own already-tested `SolarCoordinates` (right ascension/declination/apparent sidereal time) and `Astronomical.altitudeOfCelestialBody` — the same numbers this app already trusts for real prayer-time calculations — rather than a second hand-rolled implementation. Imports from the package's `lib/src/` (not part of its public API) with explicit `// ignore: implementation_imports` and a documented reason, keeping `flutter analyze` at the same 6 pre-existing infos.
- [x] The only genuinely new math is the azimuth transform (`SolarPosition.fromHourAngle`), split out as a pure function specifically so it's testable in isolation from Julian-day/sidereal-time plumbing.
- [x] **`test/solar_position_test.dart`** (new, 5 tests) — verifies the azimuth formula against a hard mathematical invariant (at solar transit, hour angle = 0, the sun is exactly due South when latitude > declination, due North when latitude < declination — checkable by construction, not a remembered textbook number) plus a qualitative sanity check (summer sun at a northern latitude sets north of due West) and a real-location smoke test. All 5 pass — this is the "make sure of its accuracy before showing it" bar the old placeholder text asked for.
- [x] **`qibla_screen.dart`**: `_QiblaMethod.sunMoon` renamed to `.sun` (moon-based finding deliberately not attempted — its usefulness depends on illumination phase and is far less reliable; not claiming a feature that isn't real). New `_SunView` (refreshes every 15s — the sun moves slowly enough that this feels live without waste): shows the sun's current azimuth/altitude, and — the actual point of this method — "قف مستقبلًا الشمس، ثم استدر Y° نحو يمينك/يسارك لتكون متجهًا نحو القبلة" computed as the signed angle between the Qibla bearing and the sun's azimuth. A shadow-method alternative is offered alongside it (shadow points opposite the sun) for anyone who can't look at the sun directly. Falls back to an honest "الشمس غاربة الآن" empty state when the sun is below the horizon (altitude < 0) rather than showing nonsense. **This method's real value**: it doesn't touch the phone's magnetometer at all, making it a genuine independent cross-check against the compass tab's known weakness (magnetic interference) — not just another view of the same sensor data.
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean (same 6 pre-existing infos), `flutter test` 220/221 (215 old + 5 new solar-position tests, same 1 pre-existing unrelated failure).
- [ ] Not yet confirmed on-device — in particular whether the "turn right/left" instruction feels intuitive in practice, and real-world accuracy against a known physical Qibla marker.

### دفعة أ — محرك تخصيص الوقت + محاكاة مونت كارلو

- [x] `session_time_budget.dart`'s `computeSessionPlan` gained optional `dueQuranReviews`/`dueKnowledgeReviews` params (default 0 — both existing call sites in `guided_session_screen.dart` unaffected unless they start passing real counts). A real backlog now greedily shifts minutes into "مراجعة سريعة" from "فهم" then "حفظ جديد", never below a 5-minute floor — deterministic, explainable, no LP solver (5 fixed phases doesn't justify one).
- [x] `lib/services/completion_forecast.dart` (new) — `CompletionForecastService.forecast()`. Key discovery: no column anywhere tracks actual minutes spent, but `memorization_progress.memorized_date` already logs a real date for every page ever memorized — grouping those gives a genuine empirical daily-pace sample. Bootstrap-resamples 2000 simulated futures from that real history, returns p10/p50/p90 days+dates; returns `insufficientData: true` (no fake range) under 10 distinct active days. `sensitivityFactors()` reuses the same simulation with two synthetic nudges (drop weakest quartile / boost strongest quartile) to rank which lever moves the median more.
- [x] Core simulation logic (`simulateDaysToComplete`/`forecastResultFromRuns`) deliberately split into pure top-level functions, not DB-touching methods — directly unit-tested in `test/completion_forecast_test.dart` (converges to an exact day-count with zero-variance input, faster samples finish sooner, percentiles always ordered). `test/session_time_budget_test.dart` covers the new backlog top-up (backward compat, floor never violated, total always exact).

## Phase 61 — مكتبة التفسير/الترجمة متعددة اللغات، الدفعة الأولى (2026-08-17)

Ismail asked for a global multi-language tafsir/translation library (سورة → آية → لغة → مفسّر). Licensing research (Phase 57's continuation, verified directly via WebFetch/WebSearch, not assumed) found QuranEnc.com's site-wide terms permit bulk export + redistribution with attribution + no modification, no commercial restriction found — the clearest legal source of the ones checked (Tanzil.net's "non-commercial" wording is ambiguous for offline bundling; Quran Foundation's API caps local caching at 7 days, unusable for permanent bundling). Planned via `EnterPlanMode` given the schema/import-pipeline decisions involved — see `quirky-gliding-shell.md`'s "مكتبة التفسير/الترجمة متعددة اللغات" section. Scoped to 2 languages first (English, Amharic) to prove the pattern before expanding — same "5 template screens" discipline as `AppMotion`'s rollout.

- [x] **DB**: migration v39→v40 adds `tafsir_entries.language TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ar'` (additive `ALTER TABLE`, safe for the 4 existing all-Arabic editions).
- [x] **`tool/fetch_quranenc_translations.dart`** (new, not part of the app — run manually via `dart run`): fetches all 114 surahs from QuranEnc's public API (`GET /api/v1/translation/sura/{key}/{n}`) for `english_rwwad` and `amharic_sadiq`, writes gzip-compressed JSONL matching the existing tafsir assets' exact shape. Actually run this session — both editions fetched successfully, full 6236/6236 ayah coverage each (`tafsir-english_rwwad.jsonl.gz` 292KB, `tafsir-amharic_sadiq.jsonl.gz` 347KB). `assets/quran/tafsir-quranenc.license.txt` records the source, editions, and QuranEnc's exact republishing terms as fetched, per their attribution requirement.
- [x] **`quran_import_service.dart`**: `_tafsirEditions` gained the 2 new entries (asset path, source key, language); import gate changed from "whole table empty → import all 4" to **per-edition self-heal** (check each `source` independently) so an existing install that already has the 4 Arabic editions still picks up the 2 new ones on next launch, not just fresh installs.
- [x] **`quran_search_repository.dart`**: `tafsirSources` extended to `(slug, label, language)` triples + new `languageLabels` map (`ar`→العربية, `en`→English, `am`→አማርኛ, each language's own name in its own script). No query changes needed — `source` was already the sole filter key.
- [x] **`quran_search_screen.dart`** + **`quran_reading_screen.dart`**: both tafsir dropdowns gained a language selector above them; picking a language re-anchors the source dropdown to that language's first edition (never lets the two fall out of sync) and re-runs the search/reload.
- [x] `pubspec.yaml` assets list updated. `flutter analyze` clean (same 6 pre-existing infos), `flutter test` 229/230 (same pre-existing failure, unchanged — no new test file needed, this is asset/data wiring not new logic).

## Phase 62 — "طابع الـ3D": Mushaf depth + PremiumModal + global design system foundation (2026-08-17)

Ismail sent a full CSS mockup ("Premium 3D Quran Reader") from ChatGPT and initially asked to copy it exactly — then explicitly corrected himself: "لا تطبق كما يقول اعمل كما تحب وما يناسبك" and "أريد فقط أن أحصل على طابع الـ3D". CSS doesn't run in Flutter; told him directly, then translated the design *intent* (layered depth, warm paper/gold, glassmorphism, floating controls, page-turn) into real Flutter mechanisms — planned via `EnterPlanMode` after 3 Explore agents confirmed the current state (`quran_reading_screen.dart` had literally zero shadow/gradient/animation on the Mushaf page; the page container's color matched the Scaffold's exactly, so there was no depth cue at all besides thin frame-painter lines; 19 files use plain-default `AlertDialog`/`showDialog`; `BackdropFilter` had never been used anywhere in the project).

- [x] **Mushaf page depth** (`quran_reading_screen.dart`): layered `BoxShadow` (soft dark lift + warm theme-color tint) finally separates the page from its surroundings. A subtle `RadialGradient` "living light" overlay is nudged by real device tilt via `RotationSensor.orientationStream` (same package `qibla_screen.dart` already uses, no new dependency; throttled to ~150ms so it doesn't spam `setState`) — replacing the web design's mouse-parallax, which doesn't exist on a touch phone. Page changes now `AnimatedSwitcher` (slide+fade, `AppMotion.premium`) for a believable page-turn instead of an instant jump. `_MushafFramePainter`/`_PageNumberCartouche` untouched — already good.
- [x] **`lib/widgets/premium_modal.dart`** (new) — `showPremiumModal`/`showPremiumDialogTransition`: `BackdropFilter` blur + layered shadow + gold-tinted border + scale/fade/rise entrance (`AppMotion.premium`/`entranceCurve`, same tokens `companion_card.dart` already uses). Applied to 3 template sites only (same "prove it first" discipline as every other design rollout this project has done): `celebration_overlay.dart` (via the bare transition helper, since its confetti+certificate content needs a full-bleed dark backdrop, not a titled card), `add_task_screen.dart`'s delete-confirm, `goals_screen.dart`'s goal form. The other 16 dialog-using files are untouched.
- [x] Ismail then explicitly widened scope: "هذا ليس خاصًا بصفحة القرآن فقط... اجعله Design System كامل للبرنامج" with creative freedom on colors per section. Built the generalized foundation: **`lib/theme/depth.dart`** (`DepthShadows` 3 tiers + `DepthPalette` 4 named section accents: quran=gold, dashboard=green, knowledge=indigo, calm=gray) and **`lib/widgets/depth_surface.dart`** (`DepthCard`/`DepthIconButton` reusable materials with physical press-in feedback). First real-screen application: `home_screen.dart`'s `_ProgressCard` gets `DepthShadows.floating`.

## Phase 63 — Language-switcher reactivity fix (2026-08-17)

Ismail pasted a large ChatGPT-sourced "enterprise localization audit" prompt (full ARB/AppLocalizations rewrite, RTL audit, locale-aware formatting, database content localization, etc.) — most of it doesn't fit a solo personal Arabic-primary religious-study app (deep Quran/hadith/tafsir content is deliberately Arabic-only or gets *real* licensed translations via the tafsir library, not machine-translated UI keys; no currency/enum/database-content localization need exists here). Investigated the actual complaint directly instead of blindly following the prompt — found 3 real, concrete bugs behind "changing language only affects a few elements":

1. **`LanguagePreferenceService.load()` was never called anywhere**, not even in `main()` — the saved language preference never actually got read back on relaunch; every launch silently reset to Arabic.
2. **`MaterialApp`'s own `locale`/`supportedLocales`/`Directionality` in `main.dart` were hardcoded to Arabic/RTL always**, completely independent of the preference service — so even a working preference could never change the app's real locale or text direction.
3. **`home_screen.dart` cached the language in a `final` field** read once at `State` construction — never refreshed after a change made on the profile screen without a full app restart.

- [x] `LanguagePreferenceService` rebuilt around a `ValueNotifier<String>` (`languageNotifier`), the same reactive pattern `TextScalePreferenceService.scaleNotifier` already established for exactly this "needs to rebuild the whole `MaterialApp` live" need. `currentLanguage` kept as a live getter mirroring the notifier for backward compatibility. Verified directly via grep of `flutter_localizations`' own generated locale list which of the 13 offered languages have real Flutter Material/Widgets/Cupertino support (`frameworkSupportedLanguages`: ar/en/am/bn/fa/fr/id/ms/sw/tr/ur — Hausa/Somali don't, they fall back to English framework chrome while the app's own `basicText()` labels still work fine for them since that's independent).
- [x] `main.dart`: added the missing `LanguagePreferenceService.load()` call; `MaterialApp` now wrapped in a second `ValueListenableBuilder<String>` (nested with the existing text-scale one) driving real `locale`/`supportedLocales`/`Directionality` (RTL for ar/ur/fa, LTR otherwise) from the live preference.
- [x] `home_screen.dart`: removed the stale `final _lang` field; the language-dependent nav labels now live inside a `ValueListenableBuilder<String>` on `languageNotifier`, guaranteed to refresh immediately regardless of navigation timing.
- [x] Scope deliberately unchanged: still "الأساسيات" only (nav/hub labels), not a full-app ARB migration — the actual bug was reactivity/wiring, not missing translation keys.
- [x] `flutter analyze` clean (same 6 pre-existing infos, no new issues).
- [ ] Not yet confirmed on-device — in particular real language switching between Arabic↔English↔Urdu (an RTL↔LTR↔RTL sequence) without restarting the app.

## Phase 64 — Notification reliability + real licensed adhan audio (Ismail's request 2026-08-17)

Ismail sent two large ChatGPT-sourced prompts: a "smart, learning-plan-aware reminder system" and an extremely detailed self-healing "Prayer/Adhan Reliability Engine" (exact OS scheduling, boot recovery, timezone/location change detection, audio fallback chains, deterministic dedup, config-hash invalidation, a diagnostics screen, heavy automated stress testing). Used `EnterPlanMode` with direct code reads (not agents — the relevant file was small enough to read in full) before writing anything. **Key finding that reshaped the whole task**: most of what both prompts asked for was already built earlier this same session (Phase 53–55) and unknown to me at the time the request arrived — `companion_engine.dart`, prayer-time-anchored adhkar reminders, a working settings screen, and 5 real prayer-time notifications all already existed and worked. Boot-recovery was also already correctly wired (`AndroidManifest.xml`'s hand-added receivers, confirmed by direct read) — this directly contradicted the ChatGPT prompt's premise that the system was Timer-based/unreliable.

**Real, confirmed gaps closed this phase:**
- [x] **Exact-alarm scheduling for prayer notifications only** — `SCHEDULE_EXACT_ALARM` permission added, `requestExactAlarmsPermission()`/`canScheduleExactNotifications()` (both already present in the pinned `flutter_local_notifications: ^22.0.1`, no new dependency) wired into `_doInit()`/`schedulePrayerTimeNotifications()`. Silently falls back to the existing `inexactAllowWhileIdle` if denied.
- [x] **Deep-linking** — every scheduled/shown notification now carries a `payload`; `main.dart` gained a global `navigatorKey` + `_routeForPayload()` mapping each payload to an existing screen (prayer times, adhkar, review, plan, Quran browse, library), handled both for taps while the app is alive (`onDidReceiveNotificationResponse`) and cold-start launches (`getNotificationAppLaunchDetails()`).
- [x] **Quiet hours** — new `lib/services/quiet_hours_prefs.dart` + pure `applyQuietHours()` function (directly unit-tested, `test/quiet_hours_test.dart`), wired into `_scheduleDailyAt`. Explicitly documented limitation: only shifts future scheduling, can't recall an alarm already fired under an earlier window.
- [x] **`lib/screens/notification_diagnostics_screen.dart`** (new) — permission states, exact-alarm status, live `pendingNotificationRequests()` listing, location status. Reachable from a new button in `notification_settings_screen.dart`.
- [x] **رفيق طالب العلم as a real push notification**, not just the in-app card — `buildCompanionContext()` extracted from `CompanionCard._load()` into `lib/services/companion_context_service.dart` for reuse; `NotificationService.scheduleOrCancelCompanionMessage()` fires only when `companionMessageFor()` returns non-null (same "says nothing when it has nothing to say" rule), called from `main.dart` on every app start.
- [x] **Real licensed adhan audio** — Ismail explicitly rejected leaving the toggle inert and supplied a specific candidate (Wikimedia Commons "Beautiful adhan.ogg"). Verified independently via `WebFetch` of the Commons file page (not just trusting the pasted claim): CC0 1.0 Universal, uploader Adam-synagda, "own work", permits commercial use/modification/redistribution with no attribution requirement. Downloaded directly from `upload.wikimedia.org`, byte size (1,229,032) cross-checked against the page's own listed size. Documented in `LICENSED_CONTENT_SOURCES.md` with source URL, license URL, and every field Ismail asked to have verified. Bundled **twice**, as required by the platform: `assets/audio/adhan_beautiful.ogg` (Flutter asset, used by a new "تجربة صوت الأذان" test-playback button via `audioplayers`) and `android/app/src/main/res/raw/adhan_beautiful.ogg` (native Android raw resource — required for notification-channel sound, a Flutter asset alone can't serve as one). `schedulePrayerTimeNotifications()` now routes to a **second** channel id (`prayer_time_notifications_adhan`) when the toggle is on, since Android locks a channel's sound at creation and silently ignores `sound:` changes on an already-created channel — reusing the same channel id would have made the toggle look like it worked while doing nothing on any device that already had the old channel.
- [x] Ismail asked to try the app rather than keep waiting on the last 6 tafsir languages (background fetch `bet14k307` still running) — temporarily commented out those 6 entries in `pubspec.yaml`, `quran_import_service.dart`'s `_tafsirEditions`, and `quran_search_repository.dart`'s `tafsirSources` (so the language picker doesn't offer a language with no imported rows yet), all three clearly marked for restoration the moment the asset files land. Everything else (41 of 47 tafsir editions, the whole notification-reliability batch, adhan audio, 3D depth foundation) shipped in this build.
